### Q01 — simple

Kérdés: How many World Drivers' Championships has Lewis Hamilton won?

Baseline válasz: Lewis Hamilton has won seven World Drivers' Championships, which ties him with Michael Schumacher for the most championships in history [S1][S3].

Baseline források: driver-lewis-hamilton#0, driver-lewis-hamilton#192, reference-formula-one#112, season-2020-formula-one-world-championship#5

After a non-scoring rookie season, he joined Renault as a test driver before his promotion to a full-time seat in 2003; he became the then-youngest polesitter and race winner at the Malaysian and Hungarian Grands Prix, respectively, before achieving several podiums across his 2004 campaign.       "text": "Vettel became BMW Sauber's third driver at the 2006 Turkish Grand Prix, when former incumbent Robert Kubica replaced Jacques Villeneuve as second driver for the 2006 Hungarian Grand Prix.       "chunk_id": "driver-sebastian-vettel#58",
      "text": ". On the opening lap, Vettel spun after an incident with Bruno Senna. ### Q03 — simple

Kérdés: Who won the 2021 Formula One World Drivers' Championship?

Baseline válasz: Max Verstappen of Red Bull Racing-Honda won the 2021 Formula One World Drivers' Championship for the first time in his career, having claimed 10 race wins across the season [S3].

### Q04 — multi-hop

Kérdés: Who was the teammate of the 2016 World Drivers' Champion at his team that season?

Baseline válasz: The 2016 World Drivers' Champion was Nico Rosberg, and his teammate that season at Mercedes was Lewis Hamilton [S1][S4].

      "description": "A Formula One driver who competed against Alonso in the 2010 season.\nSir Lewis Carl Davidson Hamilton is a British racing driver who competes in Formula One.\nBorn and raised in Stevenage, Hamilton began his career in karting at age six, winning several national titles.\nA British racing driver who has competed in Formula One.\nA Formula One driver known for his multiple World Drivers' Championships.\nA Formula One driver known for his multiple championship wins.\nA Formula One driver who raced for McLaren.\nA driver who qualified first in Hungary.\nA Formula One driver.\nHamilton was able to win with a dominant car, with a good car like 2010 or 2012, or with bad cars like 2009 and 2011.\nA Formula One driver who was teammates with Fernando Alonso.\nA driver who joined Mercedes in 2013.\nA Formula One driver who became European champion in karting.\nA Formula One driver known for his skill in managing fuel and tire usage.\nHamilton is proving to be the intellectual match of his teammate and the better racer.\nAn English Formula One driver who began karting in 1993.\nHamilton had 32 victories, 55 podium finishes, and qualified ahead of Rosberg 42 times. He won two World Championship titles.\nA Formula One driver known for his rivalry with Sebastian Vettel.\nA Formula One driver known for his competitive spirit and multiple championships.\nThe first and only black driver to race in Formula One as of 2026.\nA British Formula One driver.\nBecame the youngest driver to win the British cadet karting championship at the age of ten.\nA Formula One driver who has grown to appreciate the implications of promoting equality within the sport.\nA Formula One driver known for his achievements and public statements.\nA prominent Formula One driver, currently tied to Ferrari by a multi-year contract running through the end of 2027.\nA prominent Formula One driver known for his advocacy against racism.\nA Formula One driver who elected to compete under the car number 44.\nA Formula One driver who races with career No. 44.\nHamilton is a Catholic; he says that he prays regularly and is guided by his faith. Hamilton believes that he has the 'hand of God' resting over him when racing in Formula One.\nA British racing driver currently competing in Formula One.\nA Formula One driver known for his racing career.\nHamilton debuted at the 2007 Australian Grand Prix, becoming the first black driver to compete in Formula One. He holds over 90 records in Formula One, including the most race wins (106), pole positions (104), podium finishes (208).\nA Formula One driver known for his career with McLaren and multiple championship wins.\nA Formula One driver confirmed as McLaren's second driver.\nthe most consecutive podium finishes from debut (9), the most wins in a debut season (4, shared with Jacques Villeneuve), and the then-most points in a debut season (109). Acquiring the championship lead as early as in the fourth round of the season, Hamilton became the youngest driver to lead the World Drivers' Championship.\nthen-youngest World Drivers' Champion, and deny race-winner Massa the title by one point. Hamilton also became the first British driver to win the World Drivers' Championship since Damon Hill in 1996.\nA driver who engaged in a four-way title battle with Alonso, Sebastian Vettel, and Mark Webber throughout the season.\nFinished fourth in the standings, achieving four race wins and a season-highest seven pole positions.\nA Formula One driver who has raced for McLaren and Mercedes.\nA successful Formula One driver.\nA Formula One driver who won his fourth World Drivers' title.\nA British racing driver who has won multiple Formula One championships.\nA British racing driver competing in Formula One.\nA Formula One driver who has not secured a race win or a pole position in the 2022 season.\nA Formula One driver who experienced porpoising with the W13 car and sustained a back injury at the Azerbaijan Grand Prix.\nA Formula One driver known for his record pole positions and contract with Mercedes.\nA successful Formula One driver, ended the season a career-lowest seventh in the championship.\nprominent advocate in support of racial justice and increased diversity in motorsport. Hamilton was listed in the 2020 issue of Time as one of the 100 most influential people globally, was named the most influential black Briton in the Powerlist 2021, and was knighted by Elizabeth II in the 2021 New Year Honours.\nA British racing driver who moved from McLaren to Mercedes in 2013.\nA Formula One driver who ended the season sixth in the championship.\nRegarded as one of the most complete drivers on the grid, excelling across a wide range of areas.\nHamilton is comfortable with levels of rear instability that most other drivers would find intolerable. He has also been praised for his ability to adapt to variances in car set-up and changing track conditions; throughout his career, he has typically used less fuel than his teammates as a result of his ability to carry momentum through corners.\nHamilton has been praised for his consistency, especially in his time at Mercedes.\nA professional racing driver of Afro-Grenadian descent.\nA prominent Formula One driver known for his performances and influence in the sport.\nA Formula One driver known for his achievements and records.\nA driver known for his fierce but fair fighting style in Formula One.\nDescribed as the best driver of his generation and one of the greatest Formula One drivers.\nBritish driver in Formula One, regarded as one of the greatest British sportsmen.\nOne of the last superstar drivers, known for his jet-set lifestyle and interests outside Formula One.\nA prodigious talent as a teenager, Hamilton established himself as one of the world's best drivers.\nConsidered one of the best drivers of his generation.\nHamilton has won at least one race in fifteen consecutive seasons.\nA Formula One driver who clinched his second and third World     {
      "entity_key": "Driver:nico rosberg",
      "entity_type": "Driver",
      "name": "Nico Rosberg",
      "description": "A driver who was Hamilton's teammate at Mercedes.\nA Formula One driver who raced alongside Lewis Hamilton in karting.\nA Formula One driver who competed against Lewis Hamilton.\nRosberg was always more analytical and believed to achieve greater success in Formula One.\nRosberg had 22 victories, 50 podium finishes, and qualified ahead of Hamilton 36 times. He won one World Championship title.\nA Formula One driver and Hamilton's teammate at Mercedes from 2013 to 2016.\nA German racing driver who was Michael Schumacher's teammate at Mercedes GP.\nNico Erik Rosberg is a German and Finnish former racing driver who competed under the German flag in Formula One from 2006 to 2016.\nA Formula One driver who was Michael Schumacher's teammate at Mercedes.\nRosberg had a scientific and technical driving style; he sought to fine-tune his car in specific detail and adapted his driving ability around the changes, especially over a single lap. He shared his father's preference of oversteer over understeer and lacked a flamboyant driving technique. Rosberg's qualifying preparations would be compromised in the event that he leaned towards a race setup; he analysed the situation and attempted to optimise his driving style to win races.\nNico Rosberg is a Formula One driver who began competitive racing at the age of six.\nA driver who raced in Formula One.\nA driver who was assured of a role with Williams in 2006.\nA Formula One driver who raced for Williams.\nNico Rosberg is a Formula One driver.\nA Formula One driver known for his performances throughout the 2006-2007 seasons.\nA Formula One driver who raced for Williams during the 2008–2009 seasons.\nA Formula One driver who raced for Mercedes.\nA consistent and fast driver who was seventh in the WDC with 142 points.\nA Formula One driver who became the 2016 World Champion.\nA Formula One driver who won the championship in 2016.\nBorn on 27 June 1985, he is the son of Finnish racing driver Keke Rosberg.\nIn retirement, Rosberg's love of engineering and innovation saw him become an eco-entrepreneur, by investing in positive change to the environment and society.\nRosberg became a long-term investor and shareholder in Formula E in early 2018. Focusing primarily on Europe, Rosberg has invested in multiple start-up technology and e-mobility companies. He had the idea to launch the annual Greentech Festival trade fair, which he did with the entrepreneurs Sven Krüger and Marco Voigt in February 2019 to showcase sustainable technologies from around the world in Berlin.\nA former Formula One driver who retired after winning the championship.\nGerman driver with 1 world title.\nRosberg took an early lead in the race, and while his attempt to complete the race with only two pit stops came under threat from second-placed Jenson Button, a mistake by Button's pit crew during his final stop handed Rosberg a nineteen-second advantage over Kimi Räikkönen.\nA driver who was retained by the team for the 2015 season.",

      "text": "Rosberg's father asked CRG executive Dino Chiesa in late 1999 to establish a separate karting team for his son and fellow driver Lewis Hamilton for a period of two to three years. At the end of their season as teammates, the pair were tied\non 109 points, with Hamilton placing second and Alonso third in the World Drivers' Championship by virtue of Hamilton\nhaving more second-place finishes.\nNico Rosberg",

      "text": "# Nico Rosberg  \nNico Erik Rosberg ( NEE-koh ROZ-burg; born 27 June 1985) is a German and Finnish former racing driver, entrepreneur, and broadcaster who competed under the German flag in Formula One from 2006 to 2016. Rosberg won the Formula One World Drivers' Championship in 2016 with Mercedes, and won 23 Grands Prix across 11 seasons.",

Mercedes continued their domination in this second year of the V6 turbo era, improving on their impressive numbers from 2014 with 18 pole positions, 16 wins and 12 1–2s in 19 races",

### Q05 — multi-hop

Kérdés: Which team did the 2012 World Drivers' Champion leave his team for, and in which year did the move take effect?

Baseline válasz: The 2012 World Drivers' Champion, Kimi Räikkönen, returned to the sport after two seasons competing in the World Rally Championship, but the context does not specify which team he left or the year of his move. Therefore, I cannot answer from the provided documents.

### Q06 — multi-hop

Kérdés: Which two drivers in the corpus hold the record of seven World Championships, and with which teams did each of them win their titles?

Baseline válasz: The two drivers who hold the record of seven World Championships are Michael Schumacher and Lewis Hamilton. Michael Schumacher won his titles with Benetton and Ferrari, while Lewis Hamilton won his titles with McLaren and Mercedes [S1][S2].

Baseline források: reference-formula-one#112, reference-list-of-formula-one-world-drivers-champions#1, season-2020-formula-one-world-championship#2, season-2020-formula-one-world-championship#5

He holds over 90 records in Formula One, including the most race wins (106), pole positions (104), podium finishes (208).\nA Formula One driver known for his career with McLaren and multiple championship wins.\nA Formula One driver confirmed as McLaren's second driver.\nthe most consecutive podium finishes from debut (9), the most wins in a debut season (4, shared with Jacques Villeneuve), and the then-most points in a debut season (109). Acquiring the championship lead as early as in the fourth round of the season, Hamilton became the youngest driver to lead the World Drivers' Championship.\nthen-youngest World Drivers' Champion, and deny race-winner Massa the title by one point. Hamilton also became the first British driver to win the World Drivers' Championship since Damon Hill in 1996.\nA driver who engaged in a four-way title battle with Alonso, Sebastian Vettel, and Mark Webber throughout the season.\nFinished fourth in the standings, achieving four race wins and a season-highest seven pole positions.\nA Formula One driver who has raced for McLaren and Mercedes.\nA successful Formula One driver.\nA Formula One driver who won his fourth World Drivers' title.\nA British racing driver who has won multiple Formula One championships.\nA British racing driver competing in Formula One.\nA Formula One driver who has not secured a race win or a pole position in the 2022 season.\nA Formula One driver who experienced porpoising with the W13 car and sustained a back injury at the Azerbaijan Grand Prix.\nA Formula One driver known for his record pole positions and contract with Mercedes.\nA successful Formula One driver, ended the season a career-lowest seventh in the championship.\nprominent advocate in support of racial justice and increased diversity in motorsport. Hamilton was listed in the 2020 issue of Time as one of the 100 most influential people globally, was named the most influential black Briton in the Powerlist 2021, and was knighted by Elizabeth II in the 2021 New Year Honours.\nA British racing driver who moved from McLaren to Mercedes in 2013.\nA Formula One driver who ended the season sixth in the championship.\nRegarded as one of the most complete drivers on the grid, excelling across a wide range of areas.\nHamilton is comfortable with levels of rear instability that most other drivers would find intolerable. He has also been praised for his ability to adapt to variances in car set-up and changing track conditions; throughout his career, he has typically used less fuel than his teammates as a result of his ability to carry momentum through corners.\nHamilton has been praised for his consistency, especially in his time at Mercedes.\nA professional racing driver of Afro-Grenadian descent.\nA prominent Formula One driver known for his performances and influence in the sport.\nA Formula One driver known for his achievements and records.\nA driver known for his fierce but fair fighting style in Formula One.\nDescribed as the best driver of his generation and one of the greatest Formula One drivers.\nBritish driver in Formula One, regarded as one of the greatest British sportsmen.\nOne of the last superstar drivers, known for his jet-set lifestyle and interests outside Formula One.\nA prodigious talent as a teenager, Hamilton established himself as one of the world's best drivers.\nConsidered one of the best drivers of his generation.\nHamilton has won at least one race in fifteen consecutive seasons.\nA Formula One driver who clinched his second and third World Championship titles with Mercedes in 2014 and 2015.\nA driver regarded as one of the greatest in Formula One history, known for achieving the most all-time pole positions.\nA driver considered by some to be the greatest of his generation and potentially the greatest of all time.\nA racing driver who tied Michael Schumacher's record of seven Formula One World Drivers' Championship titles in 2020.\nreplaced Michael Schumacher at Mercedes for the 2013 season.\nA driver who raced in Formula One.\nA Formula One driver who raced for Mercedes.\nBritish driver with 7 world titles.\nA driver who passed Sebastian Vettel during the race.\nA driver who was using a two-stop strategy.\nInvolved in a collision with Romain Grosjean.\nA driver who qualified on pole.\nA driver who led the race from the start.\nA driver who fought over the race lead.\nA driver who raced for McLaren in the 2012 Formula One World Championship.\nA driver who had a slow pit stop.\nA driver who decided not to switch his car number to 1 and raced with number 44.\nA driver who raced for Mercedes.\nRaced in the 2021 Formula One World Championship.\nA driver who was leading the race comfortably.\nA driver who races ### Q07 — multi-hop

Kérdés: Fernando Alonso and Lewis Hamilton were teammates once. At which team and in which season, and what was the outcome of that season's championship?

Baseline válasz: Fernando Alonso and Lewis Hamilton were teammates at McLaren during the 2007 season. Both drivers scored four wins each and led the Drivers' Championship for much of the year, but Alonso ultimately finished the season in third place, while Hamilton finished second, losing the championship to Kimi Räikkönen. Tensions between Alonso and Hamilton were notable throughout the season, leading to Alonso's departure from the team by mutual consent in November [S1][S3].

Baseline források: team-mclaren#86, driver-fernando-alonso#48, driver-fernando-alonso#41, team-mclaren#111

Lokális válasz: A rendelkezésre álló részgráf nem támasztja alá a választ.

At the end of their season as teammates, the pair were tied\non 109 points, with Hamilton placing second and Alonso third in the World Drivers' Championship by virtue of Hamilton\nhaving more second-place finishes.\nNico Rosberg",

      "chunk_id": "driver-fernando-alonso#48",
      "text": ". McLaren's Hamilton and Button and Red Bull's of Sebastian Vettel and Mark Webber were Alonso's main championship competition. He won five races that season and entered the season-ending Abu Dhabi Grand Prix leading by eight points after being 47 behind mid-season following errors. Alonso finished runner-up to Vettel after finishing seventh thereby losing nineteen points to Vettel who won the race.",
      "text_found": true

      "chunk_id": "driver-lewis-hamilton#112",
      "text": "Luca  di  Montezemolo  regarding  a  Ferrari  drive  for  2008.  The  rivalry  between  the  pair  led  to  speculation  that  either\nHamilton or Alonso would leave McLaren at the end of the season; Alonso and McLaren terminated their contract by\nmutual consent in November that year, ending his and Hamilton's time as teammates. In subsequent years, tensions\nbetween  the  pair  dissipated,  and  the  mutual  respect  has  grown,  with  Alonso  praising  Hamilton  in  2017  saying",
      "text_found": true
    },

      "chunk_id": "driver-lewis-hamilton#68",
      "text": "\"looking  forward  to  the  end  [of  the  season]\",  Hamilton  ended  the  season  a  career-lowest  seventh  in  the\nchampionship—22  points  behind  teammate  Russell—marking  the  end  of  his  Mercedes  career,  which  was  the  most\nsuccessful driver–constructor partnership in Formula One history by several metrics.\nFerrari (2025–present)\n2025–present: First season acclimations and return to form",

### Q08 — multi-hop

Kérdés: Which driver replaced Nico Rosberg at Mercedes after his retirement, and which team did that driver come from?

Baseline válasz: Valtteri Bottas replaced Nico Rosberg at Mercedes after his retirement, and he came from the Williams team [S3].

Baseline források: team-mercedes-benz-in-formula-one#21, driver-nico-rosberg#0, team-mercedes-benz-in-formula-one#45, driver-nico-rosberg#3

Lokális válasz: A rendelkezésre álló részgráf nem támasztja alá a választ.

{
  "nodes": [
    {
      "entity_key": "Driver:nico rosberg",
      "entity_type": "Driver",
      "name": "Nico Rosberg",
      "description": "A driver who was Hamilton's teammate at Mercedes.\nA Formula One driver who raced alongside Lewis Hamilton in karting.\nA Formula One driver who competed against Lewis Hamilton.\nRosberg was always more analytical and believed to achieve greater success in Formula One.\nRosberg had 22 victories, 50 podium finishes, and qualified ahead of Hamilton 36 times. He won one World Championship title.\nA Formula One driver and Hamilton's teammate at Mercedes from 2013 to 2016.\nA German racing driver who was Michael Schumacher's teammate at Mercedes GP.\nNico Erik Rosberg is a German and Finnish former racing driver who competed under the German flag in Formula One from 2006 to 2016.\nA Formula One driver who was Michael Schumacher's teammate at Mercedes.\nRosberg had a scientific and technical driving style; he sought to fine-tune his car in specific detail and adapted his driving ability around the changes, especially over a single lap. He shared his father's preference of oversteer over understeer and lacked a flamboyant driving technique. Rosberg's qualifying preparations would be compromised in the event that he leaned towards a race setup; he analysed the situation and attempted to optimise his driving style to win races.\nNico Rosberg is a Formula One driver who began competitive racing at the age of six.\nA driver who raced in Formula One.\nA driver who was assured of a role with Williams in 2006.\nA Formula One driver who raced for Williams.\nNico Rosberg is a Formula One driver.\nA Formula One driver known for his performances throughout the 2006-2007 seasons.\nA Formula One driver who raced for Williams during the 2008–2009 seasons.\nA Formula One driver who raced for Mercedes.\nA consistent and fast driver who was seventh in the WDC with 142 points.\nA Formula One driver who became the 2016 World Champion.\nA Formula One driver who won the championship in 2016.\nBorn on 27 June 1985, he is the son of Finnish racing driver Keke Rosberg.\nIn retirement, Rosberg's love of engineering and innovation saw him become an eco-entrepreneur, by investing in positive change to the environment and society.\nRosberg became a long-term investor and shareholder in Formula E in early 2018. Focusing primarily on Europe, Rosberg has invested in multiple start-up technology and e-mobility companies. He had the idea to launch the annual Greentech Festival trade fair, which he did with the entrepreneurs Sven Krüger and Marco Voigt in February 2019 to showcase sustainable technologies from around the world in Berlin.\nA former Formula One driver who retired after winning the championship.\nGerman driver with 1 world title.\nRosberg took an early lead in the race, and while his attempt to complete the race with only two pit stops came under threat from second-placed Jenson Button, a mistake by Button's pit crew during his final stop handed Rosberg a nineteen-second advantage over Kimi Räikkönen.\nA driver who was retained by the team for the 2015 season.",

      "description": "A Formula One driver who competed against Alonso in the 2010 season.\nSir Lewis Carl Davidson Hamilton is a British racing driver who competes in Formula One.\nBorn and raised in Stevenage, Hamilton began his career in karting at age six, winning several national titles.\nA British racing driver who has competed in Formula One.\nA Formula One driver known for his multiple World Drivers' Championships.\nA Formula One driver known for his multiple championship wins.\nA Formula One driver who raced for McLaren.\nA driver who qualified first in Hungary.\nA Formula One driver.\nHamilton was able to win with a dominant car, with a good car like 2010 or 2012, or with bad cars like 2009 and 2011.\nA Formula One driver who was teammates with Fernando Alonso.\nA driver who joined Mercedes in 2013.\nA Formula One driver who became European champion in karting.\nA Formula One driver known for his skill in managing fuel and tire usage.\nHamilton is proving to be the intellectual match of his teammate and the better racer.\nAn English Formula One driver who began karting in 1993.\nHamilton had 32 victories, 55 podium finishes, and qualified ahead of Rosberg 42 times. He won two World Championship titles.\nA Formula One driver known for his rivalry with Sebastian Vettel.\nA Formula One driver known for his competitive spirit and multiple championships.\nThe first and only black driver to race in Formula One as of 2026.\nA British Formula One driver.\nBecame the youngest driver to win the British cadet karting championship at the age of ten.\nA Formula One driver who has grown to appreciate the implications of promoting equality within the sport.\nA Formula One driver known for his achievements and public statements.\nA prominent Formula One driver, currently tied to Ferrari by a multi-year contract running through the end of 2027.\nA prominent Formula One driver known for his advocacy against racism.\nA Formula One driver who elected to compete under the car number 44.\nA Formula One driver who races with career No. 44.\nHamilton is a Catholic; he says that he prays regularly and is guided by his faith. He holds over 90 records in Formula One, including the most race wins (106), pole positions (104), podium finishes (208).\nA Formula One driver known for his career with McLaren and multiple championship wins.\nA Formula One driver confirmed as McLaren's second driver.\nthe most consecutive podium finishes from debut (9), the most wins in a debut season (4, shared with Jacques Villeneuve), and the then-most points in a debut season (109). Acquiring the championship lead as early as in the fourth round of the season, Hamilton became the youngest driver to lead the World Drivers' Championship.\nthen-youngest World Drivers' Champion, and deny race-winner Massa the title by one point.       "description": "A German racing driver who is a seven-time Formula One World Champion.\nA German former racing driver who competed in Formula One from 1991 to 2006 and from 2010 to 2012.\nA Formula One driver known for his achievements and records.\nA German racing driver who returned to Formula One for the 2010 season.\nA driver who won seven World Championships.\nA Formula One driver who signed a three-year contract with Mercedes.\nsecond driver in history (after Barrichello) to race in 300 Grands Prix; he took seventh place after starting 13th.\nClosed his career with 91 wins, 155 podiums, and 68 pole positions, which were all records at the time.\nFormula One driver, and the records which he holds in our sport speak volumes for his success and commitment.\nA driver who raced in Formula One.\nNoted throughout his career for his speed and racecraft, and his ability to produce fast laps at crucial moments in a race and to push his car to the very limit for sustained periods.\nSchumacher exercised four hours a day, mostly to strengthen his neck muscles to better withstand G-forces during races. In 2003, Deutsche Welle highlighted Schumacher's 'natural talent' for racing and his 'discipline and leadership'.       "text": "# Nico Rosberg  \nNico Erik Rosberg ( NEE-koh ROZ-burg; born 27 June 1985) is a German and Finnish former racing driver, entrepreneur, and broadcaster who competed under the German flag in Formula One from 2006 to 2016. Rosberg won the Formula One World Drivers' Championship in 2016 with Mercedes, and won 23 Grands Prix across 11 seasons.",
      "text_found": true
    },
    {
      "chunk_id": "driver-nico-rosberg#18",
      "text": "Rosberg's father asked CRG executive Dino Chiesa in late 1999 to establish a separate karting team for his son and fellow driver Lewis Hamilton for a period of two to three years. Rosberg raced for TeamMBM.com (Mercedes-Benz McLaren) for the 2000 season. In Formula A, he was runner-up in the CIK-FIA European Championship and finished ninth in the World Cup. In 2001, Rosberg was tenth in the South Garda Winter Cup and sixteenth in the Formula Super A World Championship",
      "text_found": true
    }

## Q09–Q13

Ezek további kérdések, amelyeket a Q01–Q08 dokumentumaiból kijelölt körön futtatunk. A saját dokumentumlistájuk szélesebb, és nincs benne a körben.

### Q09 — global

Kérdés: Across all the drivers covered in this corpus, who has won the most World Drivers' Championships, and how many?

Baseline válasz: The drivers who have won the most World Drivers' Championships are Michael Schumacher and Lewis Hamilton, each with seven championships [S1][S2].

Baseline források: reference-formula-one#112, reference-list-of-formula-one-world-drivers-champions#1, season-2020-formula-one-world-championship#2, reference-formula-one#113

Lokális válasz: A rendelkezésre álló részgráf nem támasztja alá a választ.

      "description": "A Formula One driver who competed against Alonso in the 2010 season.\nSir Lewis Carl Davidson Hamilton is a British racing driver who competes in Formula One.\nBorn and raised in Stevenage, Hamilton began his career in karting at age six, winning several national titles.\nA British racing driver who has competed in Formula One.\nA Formula One driver known for his multiple World Drivers' Championships.\nA Formula One driver known for his multiple championship wins.\nA Formula One driver who raced for McLaren.\nA driver who qualified first in Hungary.\nA Formula One driver.\nHamilton was able to win with a dominant car, with a good car like 2010 or 2012, or with bad cars like 2009 and 2011.\nA Formula One driver who was teammates with Fernando Alonso.\nA driver who joined Mercedes in 2013.\nA Formula One driver who became European champion in karting.\nA Formula One driver known for his skill in managing fuel and tire usage.\nHamilton is proving to be the intellectual match of his teammate and the better racer.\nAn English Formula One driver who began karting in 1993.\nHamilton had 32 victories, 55 podium finishes, and qualified ahead of Rosberg 42 times. He won two World Championship titles.\nA Formula One driver known for his rivalry with Sebastian Vettel.\nA Formula One driver known for his competitive spirit and multiple championships.\nThe first and only black driver to race in Formula One as of 2026.\nA British Formula One driver.\nBecame the youngest driver to win the British cadet karting championship at the age of ten.\nA Formula One driver who has grown to appreciate the implications of promoting equality within the sport.\nA Formula One driver known for his achievements and public statements.\nA prominent Formula One driver, currently tied to Ferrari by a multi-year contract running through the end of 2027.\nA prominent Formula One driver known for his advocacy against racism.\nA Formula One driver who elected to compete under the car number 44.\nA Formula One driver who races with career No. 44.\nHamilton is a Catholic; he says that he prays regularly and is guided by his faith. Hamilton believes that he has the 'hand of God' resting over him when racing in Formula One.\nA British racing driver currently competing in Formula One.\nA Formula One driver known for his racing career.\nHamilton debuted at the 2007 Australian Grand Prix, becoming the first black driver to compete in Formula One. He holds over 90 records in Formula One, including the most race wins (106), pole positions (104), podium finishes (208).\nA Formula One driver known for his career with McLaren and multiple championship wins.\nA Formula One driver confirmed as McLaren's second driver.\nthe most consecutive podium finishes from debut (9), the most wins in a debut season (4, shared with Jacques Villeneuve), and the then-most points in a debut season (109). Acquiring the championship lead as early as in the fourth round of the season, Hamilton became the youngest driver to lead the World Drivers' Championship.\nthen-youngest World Drivers' Champion, and deny race-winner Massa the title by one point. Hamilton also became the first British driver to win the World Drivers' Championship since Damon Hill in 1996.\nA driver who engaged in a four-way title battle with Alonso, Sebastian Vettel, and Mark Webber throughout the season.\nFinished fourth in the standings, achieving four race wins and a season-highest seven pole positions.\nA Formula One driver who has raced for McLaren and Mercedes.\nA successful Formula One driver.\nA Formula One driver who won his fourth World Drivers' title.\nA British racing driver who has won multiple Formula One championships.\nA British racing driver competing in Formula One.\nA Formula One driver who has not secured a race win or a pole position in the 2022 season.\nA Formula One driver who experienced porpoising with the W13 car and sustained a back injury at the Azerbaijan Grand Prix.\nA Formula One driver known for his record pole positions and contract with Mercedes.\nA successful Formula One driver, ended the season a career-lowest seventh in the championship.\nprominent advocate in support of racial justice and increased diversity in motorsport. Hamilton was listed in the 2020 issue of Time as one of the 100 most influential people globally, was named the most influential black Briton in the Powerlist 2021, and was knighted by Elizabeth II in the 2021 New Year Honours.\nA British racing driver who moved from McLaren to Mercedes in 2013.\nA Formula One driver who ended the season sixth in the championship.\nRegarded as one of the most complete drivers on the grid, excelling across a wide range of areas.\nHamilton is comfortable with levels of rear instability that most other drivers would find intolerable. He has also been praised for his ability to adapt to variances in car set-up and changing track conditions; throughout his career, he has typically used less fuel than his teammates as a result of his ability to carry momentum through corners.\nHamilton has been praised for his consistency, especially in his time at Mercedes.\nA professional racing driver of Afro-Grenadian descent.\nA prominent Formula One driver known for his performances and influence in the sport.\nA Formula One driver known for his achievements and records.\nA driver known for his fierce but fair fighting style in Formula One.\nDescribed as the best driver of his generation and one of the greatest Formula One drivers.\nBritish driver in Formula One, regarded as one of the greatest British sportsmen.\nOne of the last superstar drivers, known for his jet-set lifestyle and interests outside Formula One.\nA prodigious talent as a teenager, Hamilton established himself as one of the world's best drivers.\nConsidered one of the best drivers of his generation.\nHamilton has won at least one race in fifteen consecutive seasons.\nA Formula One driver who clinched his second and third World Championship titles with Mercedes in 2014 and 2015.\nA driver regarded as one of the greatest in Formula One history, known for achieving the most all-time pole positions.\nA driver considered by some       "entity_key": "Driver:nico rosberg",
      "entity_type": "Driver",
      "name": "Nico Rosberg",
      "description": "A driver who was Hamilton's teammate at Mercedes.\nA Formula One driver who raced alongside Lewis Hamilton in karting.\nA Formula One driver who competed against Lewis Hamilton.\nRosberg was always more analytical and believed to achieve greater success in Formula One.\nRosberg had 22 victories, 50 podium finishes, and qualified ahead of Hamilton 36 times. He won one World Championship title.\nA Formula One driver and Hamilton's teammate at Mercedes from 2013 to 2016.\nA German racing driver who was Michael Schumacher's teammate at Mercedes GP.\nNico Erik Rosberg is a German and Finnish former racing driver who competed under the German flag in Formula One from 2006 to 2016.\nA Formula One driver who was Michael Schumacher's teammate at Mercedes.\nRosberg had a scientific and technical driving style; he sought to fine-tune his car in specific detail and adapted his driving ability around the changes, especially over a single lap. He shared his father's preference of oversteer over understeer and lacked a flamboyant driving technique. Rosberg's qualifying preparations would be compromised in the event that he leaned towards a race setup; he analysed the situation and attempted to optimise his driving style to win races.\nNico Rosberg is a Formula One driver who began competitive racing at the age of six.\nA driver who raced in Formula One.\nA driver who was assured of a role with Williams in 2006.\nA Formula One driver who raced for Williams.\nNico Rosberg is a Formula One driver.\nA Formula One driver known for his performances throughout the 2006-2007 seasons.\nA Formula One driver who raced for Williams during the 2008–2009 seasons.\nA Formula One driver who raced for Mercedes.\nA consistent and fast driver who was seventh in the WDC with 142 points.\nA Formula One driver who became the 2016 World Champion.\nA Formula One driver who won the championship in 2016.\nBorn on 27 June 1985, he is the son of Finnish racing driver Keke Rosberg.\nIn retirement, Rosberg's love of engineering and innovation saw him become an eco-entrepreneur, by investing in positive change to the environment and society.\nRosberg became a long-term investor and shareholder in Formula E in early 2018. Focusing primarily on Europe, Rosberg has invested in multiple start-up technology and e-mobility companies. He had the idea to launch the annual Greentech Festival trade fair, which he did with the entrepreneurs Sven Krüger and Marco Voigt in February 2019 to showcase sustainable technologies from around the world in Berlin.\nA former Formula One driver who retired after winning the championship.\nGerman driver with 1 world title.\nRosberg took an early lead in the race, and while his attempt to complete the race with only two pit stops came under threat from second-placed Jenson Button, a mistake by Button's pit crew during his final stop handed Rosberg a nineteen-second advantage over Kimi Räikkönen.\nA driver who was retained by the team for the 2015 season.",

An Fédération Internationale de l'Automobile (FIA; F1's governing body)-imposed ban on Renault's tuned mass damper device to slow Alonso and an increase of development into Schumacher's Ferrari for competitiveness saw the two tied on points entering the season's penultimate round, the Japanese Grand Prix.       "text": ". Ferrari's fears were not without merit as the drivers starting from even-number spaces fell behind at the start of the race",

### Q12 — global

Kérdés: Group the World Champions among the corpus drivers by decade (2000s, 2010s, 2020s).

Baseline válasz: Based on the provided context, the World Champions among the corpus drivers can be grouped by decade as follows:

**2000s:**
- Michael Schumacher (7 titles)
- Fernando Alonso (2 titles)

**2010s:**
- Lewis Hamilton (7 titles)
- Nico Rosberg (1 title)
- Sebastian Vettel (4 titles)

**2020s:**
- Max Verstappen (4 titles)

This grouping is based on the information from the drivers' titles in the context [S3].

Baseline források: season-2020-formula-one-world-championship#2, reference-formula-one#111, reference-list-of-formula-one-world-drivers-champions#1, season-2020-formula-one-world-championship#8

      "text": ". On the opening lap, Vettel spun after an incident with Bruno Senna.       "chunk_id": "driver-valtteri-bottas#14",
      "text": "Bottas was signed as the official Formula One test driver for the Williams team in January 2010 after his first year in the Formula 3 Euro Series. Team principal Frank Williams praised Bottas's record in the junior categories and expressed his hope that the role would \"provide him with another step in his development\". He conducted straight-line aerodynamic testing in the team's FW32 during the year",
      "text_found": true
    },
    {
      "chunk_id": "driver-valtteri-bottas#34",
      "text": ". He qualified fourth behind Massa at the British Grand Prix and the two drivers progressed to first and second place in the opening laps. Bottas later commented that he \"would have been able to pull a gap\" had Massa conceded his position, but the two ultimately dropped back to fourth and fifth by the end. Despite this, the result promoted Bottas to fourth place in the World Drivers' Championship.",
      "text_found": true
    }
  ]
}

      "description": "A Formula One driver who competed against Alonso in the 2010 season.\nSir Lewis Carl Davidson Hamilton is a British racing driver who competes in Formula One.\nBorn and raised in Stevenage, Hamilton began his career in karting at age six, winning several national titles.\nA British racing driver who has competed in Formula One.\nA Formula One driver known for his multiple World Drivers' Championships.\nA Formula One driver known for his multiple championship wins.\nA Formula One driver who raced for McLaren.\nA driver who qualified first in Hungary.\nA Formula One driver.\nHamilton was able to win with a dominant car, with a good car like 2010 or 2012, or with bad cars like 2009 and 2011.\nA Formula One driver who was teammates with Fernando Alonso.\nA driver who joined Mercedes in 2013.\nA Formula One driver who became European champion in karting.\nA Formula One driver known for his skill in managing fuel and tire usage.\nHamilton is proving to be the intellectual match of his teammate and the better racer.\nAn English Formula One driver who began karting in 1993.\nHamilton had 32 victories, 55 podium finishes, and qualified ahead of Rosberg 42 times. He won two World Championship titles.\nA Formula One driver known for his rivalry with Sebastian Vettel.\nA Formula One driver known for his competitive spirit and multiple championships.\nThe first and only black driver to race in Formula One as of 2026.\nA British Formula One driver.\nBecame the youngest driver to win the British cadet karting championship at the age of ten.\nA Formula One driver who has grown to appreciate the implications of promoting equality within the sport.\nA Formula One driver known for his achievements and public statements.\nA prominent Formula One driver, currently tied to Ferrari by a multi-year contract running through the end of 2027.\nA prominent Formula One driver known for his advocacy against racism.\nA Formula One driver who elected to compete under the car number 44.\nA Formula One driver who races with career No. 44.\nHamilton is a Catholic; he says that he prays regularly and is guided by his faith. Hamilton believes that he has the 'hand of God' resting over him when racing in Formula One.\nA British racing driver currently competing in Formula One.\nA Formula One driver known for his racing career.\nHamilton debuted at the 2007 Australian Grand Prix, becoming the first black driver to compete in Formula One. He holds over 90 records in Formula One, including the most race wins (106), pole positions (104), podium finishes (208).\nA Formula One driver known for his career with McLaren and multiple championship wins.\nA Formula One driver confirmed as McLaren's second driver.\nthe most consecutive podium finishes from debut (9), the most wins in a debut season (4, shared with Jacques Villeneuve), and the then-most points in a debut season (109). Acquiring the championship lead as early as in the fourth round of the season, Hamilton became the youngest driver to lead the World Drivers' Championship.\nthen-youngest World Drivers' Champion, and deny race-winner Massa the title by one point. Hamilton also became the first British driver to win the World Drivers' Championship since Damon Hill in 1996.\nA driver who engaged in a four-way title battle with Alonso, Sebastian Vettel, and Mark Webber throughout the season.\nFinished fourth in the standings, achieving four race wins and a season-highest seven pole positions.\nA Formula One driver who has raced for McLaren and Mercedes.\nA successful Formula One driver.\nA Formula One driver who won his fourth World Drivers' title.\nA British racing driver who has won multiple Formula One championships.\nA British racing driver competing in Formula One.\nA Formula One driver who has not secured a race win or a pole position in the 2022 season.\nA Formula One driver who experienced porpoising with the W13 car and sustained a back injury at the Azerbaijan Grand Prix.\nA Formula One driver known for his record pole positions and contract with Mercedes.\nA successful Formula One driver, ended the season a career-lowest seventh in the championship.\nprominent advocate in support of racial justice and increased diversity in motorsport. Hamilton was listed in the 2020 issue of Time as one of the 100 most influential people globally, was named the most influential black Briton in the Powerlist 2021, and was knighted by Elizabeth II in the 2021 New Year Honours.\nA British racing driver who moved from McLaren to Mercedes in 2013.\nA Formula One driver who ended the season sixth in the championship.\nRegarded as one of the most complete drivers on the grid, excelling across a wide range of areas.\nHamilton is comfortable with levels of rear instability that most other drivers would find intolerable. He has also been praised for his ability to adapt to variances in car set-up and changing track conditions; throughout his career, he has typically used less fuel than his teammates as a result of his ability to carry momentum through corners.\nHamilton has been praised for his consistency, especially in his time at Mercedes.\nA professional racing driver of Afro-Grenadian descent.\nA prominent Formula One driver known for his performances and influence in the sport.\nA Formula One driver known for his achievements and records.\nA driver known for his fierce but fair fighting style in last superstar drivers, known for his jet-set lifestyle and interests outside Formula One.\nA prodigious talent as a teenager, Hamilton established himself as one of the world's best       "text": "After switching to Red Bull in 2009, Vettel regularly used a variety of new helmet designs. Some designs were small changes to his original Red Bull design, while others were completely new designs, such as the one he used at the 2010 Japanese Grand Prix: Vettel had a special white-red helmet design, with black kanji and hiragana for \"gives you wings\".       "text": ". His title hopes were dealt another blow in Malaysia, as he started last following a turbo problem in qualifying",

      "text": "One week before the Canadian Grand Prix, the FIA declared the floor used by Red Bull in Monaco to be illegal, forcing the team to change the offending parts for the Canadian Grand Prix. Értékelés: A lánc elutasította a választ, mert a részgráf nem volt elég.

## Értékelési szabály

A Q04–Q08 kérdések multi-hopok, a Q01–Q03 nem. A baseline ezeken a top-4 részletből gyakran olyan szezonrészletet hozott, amelyet a kérdés nem nevezett meg. A lokális lánc lefutott. Ahol a válasz az elutasító mondat, ott a részgráf nem adott elég támaszt. A kiválasztott forrásszöveg a gráf állítását csak akkor igazolja, ha a részletben benne van; az él leírása önmagában nem forrásszöveg. Fölény nincs állítva.
